# TODO — remaining work

Scope of the full project (from the approved plan): **full ECMAScript 2026 + transpile arbitrary `node_modules`**.

Items are grouped by how soon they unblock the most user value. Within each group the ordering reflects dependency order and implementation leverage. Completed work belongs in [`done.md`](done.md), not in this TODO file.

---

## 1. Next-up unblockers

- Verified bounded AbortSignal default-reason and exception-value coverage: `AbortController.abort()` and `abort(undefined)` preserve an error-shaped `AbortError` reason with Node-compatible message and code `20`, while synchronous `throwIfAborted()` preserves that identity plus explicit `null` and custom object reasons through typed `catch (...: any | unknown)` bindings, including `finally`/rethrow paths; propagation through timers, filesystem Promise pre-aborts, and native net/HTTP/child-process cancellation remains intact, while deferred async/lazy continuation catches and unannotated string bindings remain on the compatibility string bridge. Tests: `abort_controller_default_reason`, `net_connect_signal`.
- Verified bounded HTTP header mutation/introspection coverage: `ClientRequest` and `ServerResponse` now expose case-insensitive `setHeader` / `getHeader` / `hasHeader` / `removeHeader` methods with validation, replacement, and pre-send mutation guards, while both objects synchronize `headersSent` when request or response headers are written. Test: `http_header_mutation`.
- Verified bounded HTTP trailer coverage: chunked `ClientRequest.addTrailers(...)` and `ServerResponse.addTrailers(...)` emit validated trailer fields, server `IncomingMessage.trailers` exposes decoded request trailers, and client responses expose decoded response trailers while preserving empty-trailer behavior. Test: `http_trailers`.
- Verified bounded HTTP client timeout coverage: `ClientRequest.setTimeout(timeout, callback?)` forwards the native socket idle timer, emits the request `timeout` event, invokes the optional callback, returns the request for chaining, and clears the bridge when the response completes or request is destroyed. Broader agent, proxy, and full stream-timeout semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_timeout`.
- Verified bounded HTTP server timeout coverage: `ServerResponse.setTimeout(timeout, callback?)` forwards the native socket idle timer, emits the response `timeout` event, invokes the optional callback, returns the response for chaining, and clears the bridge when the response ends. Broader agent, proxy, and full stream-timeout semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_response_timeout`.
- Verified bounded HTTP client socket-options coverage: `ClientRequest.setNoDelay(noDelay?)` and `setSocketKeepAlive(enable?, initialDelay?)` forward to the native POSIX socket controls, preserve chainable request returns, and retain underlying validation. Broader agent, proxy, and full socket-option semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_socket_options`.
- Verified bounded HTTP client abort coverage: `ClientRequest.abort()` now performs one-shot request cancellation through the existing destroy lifecycle, exposes synchronized `aborted` state, emits one `abort` event, and remains chainable. Broader abort and agent semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_abort`.
- Verified bounded HTTP client destroy error/callback coverage: `ClientRequest.destroy(error?, callback?)` now preserves a supplied dynamic error, emits one `error` before teardown, invokes an optional callback after teardown including on repeated calls, and remains chainable. Broader agent and stream-lifecycle semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_destroy_error`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server.listen` AbortSignal coverage: options-form POSIX servers accept pre-aborted and in-flight cancellation, defer cancellation to the server poll so listeners receive the signal reason as one error before close, and preserve synchronized `listening` state. Test: `net_server_signal`.
- Verified bounded `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` AbortSignal coverage: options-form POSIX sockets accept pre-aborted and in-flight cancellation, defer cancellation to the socket poll so listeners receive the signal reason as one error before close, and preserve destroyed lifecycle state. Test: `net_connect_signal`.
- Verified bounded `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` socket options coverage: options-form POSIX sockets validate and apply `noDelay`, `keepAlive`, and `keepAliveInitialDelay` before connecting while preserving existing endpoint and lifecycle behavior. Test: `net_connect_socket_options`.
- Verified bounded `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` family options coverage: options-form POSIX sockets validate `family` values `0`, `4`, and `6`, constrain host resolution to the requested IPv4/IPv6 family, and preserve endpoint-family metadata. Test: `net_connect_family_options`.
- Verified bounded `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` timeout options coverage: options-form POSIX sockets validate a non-negative finite `timeout`, start the existing inactivity timer after `connect`, emit one `timeout` event, and preserve explicit socket timeout behavior. Test: `net_connect_timeout_options`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server` listening-close lifecycle coverage: closing a POSIX TCP server from its `listening` callback now stops the active poll before the closed descriptor can be accepted again. Test: `net_server_close_on_listening`.
- Verified bounded `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` local endpoint options coverage: POSIX sockets validate and bind `{ localAddress?, localPort? }` before connecting, support wildcard-family binding when only a local port is supplied, preserve endpoint metadata, and reject invalid local endpoint values. Test: `net_connect_local_options`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server.listen({ ipv6Only })` coverage: IPv6 options-form servers validate the boolean option and configure native `IPV6_V6ONLY` behavior while preserving existing IPv4/IPv6 defaults. Test: `net_server_listen_ipv6_only`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server.listen({ backlog })` coverage: options-form servers validate a finite non-negative integer backlog and pass it to the native POSIX listen queue while preserving the existing default. Test: `net_server_listen_backlog`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` / `net.Server` `ref()` / `unref()` coverage: POSIX socket and server polling handles now participate in event-loop liveness, socket idle-timeout handles preserve their unref state when recreated, and `ref()` restores the default keep-alive behavior. Tests: `net_socket_ref_unref`, `net_server_ref_unref`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket.destroy(callback?)` coverage: POSIX TCP sockets invoke a one-shot destroy callback from the close lifecycle, including already-closed sockets, while preserving synchronized destroyed and ready-state properties. Error-object destruction remains deferred. Test: `net_socket_destroy_callback`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket.destroy(error, callback?)` coverage: POSIX TCP sockets emit a supplied error value before the close lifecycle and then invoke the one-shot destroy callback with synchronized destroyed and ready-state properties. Test: `net_socket_destroy_error`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server.getConnections(...)` coverage: POSIX TCP servers asynchronously report a null error and live accepted-connection count through the existing event loop. Test: `net_server_get_connections`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server.maxConnections` coverage: POSIX TCP servers expose an assignment-compatible `maxConnections` limit (`0` for unlimited), reject accepted sockets beyond a positive safe-integer cap before emitting `connection`, and preserve the live `connections` count. Test: `net_server_max_connections`.
- Verified bounded `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` options coverage: POSIX IPv4 sockets accept `{ port, host? }` option objects through both aliases, preserve callback and endpoint behavior, and default an omitted host to loopback. Test: `net_connect_options`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server.listen` options coverage: POSIX IPv4 servers accept `{ port, host? }` option objects with listening callbacks and preserve the existing address and close lifecycle. Test: `net_server_listen_options`.
- Verified bounded IPv6 net transport coverage: POSIX TCP servers and `net.connect` / `net.createConnection` resolve IPv4/IPv6 hosts, bind and connect with the selected address family, and expose IPv6 server/socket endpoint metadata while preserving IPv4 defaults. Test: `net_ipv6_tcp`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` backpressure coverage: writes above the 16 KiB high-water mark queue through the timer-polled socket, return `false`, expose synchronized `writableHighWaterMark` / `writableLength` / `writableNeedDrain` state, invoke queued write callbacks, emit `drain`, and defer `end()` completion until queued bytes flush. Test: `net_socket_backpressure`.
- Verified bounded chunked HTTP `ServerResponse` backpressure coverage: explicit streaming writes propagate the underlying 16 KiB socket high-water mark, expose synchronized `writableHighWaterMark` / `writableLength` / `writableNeedDrain` state, provide response EventEmitter methods, and forward `drain`; the shared HTTP zero-chunk terminator now emits its exact five-byte wire form. Test: `http_response_backpressure`.
- Verified bounded chunked HTTP `ClientRequest` backpressure coverage: queued writes report `false` at the 16 KiB high-water mark, expose synchronized `writableHighWaterMark` / `writableLength` / `writableNeedDrain` state before and after connection, and forward socket `drain` events. Test: `http_request_backpressure`.
- Verified bounded sequential chunked HTTP keep-alive pooling: framed chunked `ClientRequest` writes and chunked responses now reuse one endpoint-keyed idle connection, preserve exact server-side chunk consumption, and cleanly discard close-delimited or non-reusable pooled sockets. Concurrent pooling, pipelining, and broader streaming remain deferred. Test: `http_chunked_keep_alive_pool`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` completion coverage: POSIX TCP sockets now invoke `write()` and `end()` callbacks after native completion and emit one `finish` event when the writable half closes. Test: `net_socket_write_callbacks`.
- Verified bounded `net.Server` state coverage: POSIX TCP servers now expose synchronized `listening` and live `connections` properties across listen, accept, socket close, and server close transitions. Test: `net_server_state`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` state coverage: POSIX TCP sockets now expose synchronized `readable`, `writable`, `readableEnded`, and `writableEnded` flags plus `localFamily` / `remoteFamily` endpoint metadata. Test: `net_socket_state`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` pause/resume coverage: POSIX TCP sockets now expose chainable `pause()` / `resume()` read gates, leaving incoming data unread while paused and delivering it after resume. Test: `net_socket_pause_resume`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` timeout coverage: POSIX TCP sockets now expose chainable `setTimeout(timeout, callback?)`, emit the "timeout" event after inactivity, reset the idle timer on successful socket activity, and cancel the timer when disabled or closed. Test: `net_socket_timeout`.
- Verified bounded `net.Socket` option and counter coverage: POSIX TCP sockets now expose `bytesRead` / `bytesWritten`, apply `setNoDelay(noDelay?)` through `TCP_NODELAY`, and apply `setKeepAlive(enable?, initialDelay?)` through `SO_KEEPALIVE` with platform TCP idle-delay support while preserving chainable socket methods. - Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield nested binary-spread coverage: bounded non-assignment binary string-composition expressions can stage direct yielded operands as typed and dynamic array spread sources; assignment, nested non-direct, and broader spread graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_nested_binary_spread_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield conditional-spread coverage: supported conditional selector expressions containing direct yielded operands can stage typed and dynamic array/object spread sources while preserving selector laziness, yield order, and final spread expansion; deeper conditional graphs embedded inside non-conditional branch expressions, nested yielded operands, yielded logical right operands, assignments, and broader selector graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_conditional_spread_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield conditional-selector coverage: terminal conditional expressions can recursively stage direct yielded operands through supported prefix, comparison, logical-left, call, and nested-conditional selector forms before evaluating yield-free branches; yielded branches, yielded logical right operands, assignments, and broader selector graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_conditional_spread_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield conditional-branch spread coverage: one conditional with a direct yielded selector and one or more direct yielded operands per branch can stage only the selected typed/dynamic array or object spread source while preserving branch laziness and final spread expansion; deeper conditional graphs embedded inside non-conditional branch expressions, nested yielded branch expressions, assignments, and broader graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_conditional_branch_spread_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield nested conditional-branch spread coverage: a conditional branch can recursively stage a nested conditional arm when each selector is a direct yield and each non-conditional leaf contains one or more direct yielded operands, preserving selected-path laziness and final spread expansion; deeper conditional graphs embedded inside non-conditional branch expressions, nested yielded branch operands, assignments, and broader graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_nested_conditional_branch_spread_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield embedded-conditional coverage: terminal array/object expressions can stage one supported conditional subtree embedded between direct yielded operands, preserving source-order resumption before and after the selected branch while keeping the unselected branch lazy; deeper conditional graphs embedded inside non-conditional branch expressions, nested yielded branch operands, assignments, and broader graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_embedded_conditional_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield sibling-conditional coverage: terminal array/object expressions can stage multiple sibling conditional roots in source order, with each direct-yield selector and supported branch operands remaining lazy until selected while surrounding direct yields retain their ordering; deeper conditional graphs embedded inside non-conditional branch expressions, nested yielded branch operands, assignments, and broader graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_sibling_conditional_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield nested embedded-conditional coverage: terminal array/object expressions can stage a nested conditional event inside a selected non-conditional branch expression, preserving direct yielded operands before and after the nested selector while retaining outer and inner branch laziness; deeper conditional graphs, nested yielded operands, assignments, and broader expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_nested_embedded_conditional_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield conditional-empty-arm coverage: conditional terminal expressions can route a selected yield-free branch directly to final construction while suspending only direct yields in the other branch, preserving branch laziness and final-resume values; deeper conditional graphs, nested yielded operands, assignments, and broader expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_conditional_empty_branch_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield logical-right coverage: terminal logical expressions can stage direct yielded left and right operands, suspending the right operand only when `&&`, `||`, or `??` requires it and preserving short-circuit result values; yield-free nested operands, broader logical graphs, and other expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_logical_right_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield nested-logical coverage: terminal logical expressions can recursively stage nested `&&`, `||`, and `??` trees whose operands are direct yields, preserving nested short-circuit timing and final result values; yield-free nested operands and logical graphs in other unsupported expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_nested_logical_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield embedded-logical coverage: array/object terminal expressions can stage source-ordered recursive logical plans alongside surrounding direct yields, preserving short-circuit timing across typed arrays, dynamic objects, and sibling logical plans; non-literal yield-free nested operands and logical graphs in other unsupported expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_embedded_logical_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield logical-literal coverage: logical plans can include simple literal leaves alongside direct yielded operands, preserving no-suspension literal short-circuit branches in terminal roots and array/object returns; non-literal yield-free operands, conditional mixtures, and broader logical graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_logical_literal_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield conditional-logical coverage: conditional terminal expressions can stage recursive direct-yield logical plans inside selected conditional arms, preserving branch laziness, nested short-circuit timing, and source order around the conditional; unrelated yielded branch graphs and broader conditional mixtures remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_conditional_logical_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield logical conditional-selector coverage: conditional terminal expressions can stage recursive direct-yield logical selector conditions before choosing yield-bearing arms, preserving selector short-circuit timing and selected-branch laziness; broader selector graphs and unrelated conditional mixtures remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_logical_conditional_selector_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield object-method coverage: dynamic and named typed object terminal returns can emit static-key, yield-free methods and non-async generator methods, including generic control-flow bodies handled by the existing generator closure path, at final resume while preserving nested generator invocation; async generator methods, unsupported closure shapes, yielded spreads, computed method keys, and broader expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_method_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield object-accessor coverage: dynamic object terminal returns can emit static-key, yield-free getters and setters at final resume, preserving accessor descriptor creation and post-resume getter/setter behavior; yielded accessor bodies, typed accessor fields, and broader expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_method_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield computed-key coverage: dynamic object terminal returns can evaluate yield-free computed property keys during final construction and preserve key-before-value assignment ordering; typed object computed fields, yielded key expressions, and broader expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_computed_key_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield call/new coverage: finite ordered direct `yield` arguments can be staged alongside yield-free arguments before a call or constructor runs on the final resume; spread arguments, yielded callees, nested yielded operands, and broader expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_call_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield template coverage: standard and identifier-tagged templates can stage finite ordered direct `yield` substitutions before final formatting; nested yielded substitutions and yielded tags remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_template_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield conditional coverage: a direct `yield` can stage a conditional selector before final resume, then evaluate only the selected yield-free branch; yielded branches and broader conditional expression graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_conditional_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield logical coverage: a direct `yield` on the left of `&&`, `||`, or `??` can suspend before a yield-free right operand, preserving final-resume short-circuit and nullish timing; nested yielded right operands and broader logical graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_logical_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation coverage: direct yielded receivers and direct yielded computed keys can stage before prefix/postfix property updates and `delete`, preserving receiver-then-key and final-resume mutation timing; yielded operands outside this receiver/key subset and broader mutation graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield assignment coverage: direct yielded receivers and computed keys can stage before a direct yielded right-hand side assigns to an identifier or dynamic-property lvalue, including compound assignment; broader yielded lvalues and assignment graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_assignment_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield terminal assignment-RHS coverage: a terminal assignment can stage a supported non-assignment RHS expression tree containing multiple direct yields after the yielded receiver and computed key, preserving receiver/key/RHS order and final assignment value; broader lvalue/assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_assignment_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield terminal nested-assignment-RHS coverage: a terminal assignment can recursively stage nested assignment RHS graphs when each nested lvalue uses supported yielded receiver/key operands and each RHS uses direct yields, preserving source-order suspension and the final assignment result; deeper or unsupported assignment graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_assignment_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield terminal assignment-spread-RHS coverage: a terminal assignment RHS can stage one direct yielded array/object spread source when it is the final yield-bearing literal operand, materializing the spread before a later outer yield and preserving spread side effects and final assignment order; non-final or multiple spreads, unsupported iterable shapes, and broader lvalue/assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_assignment_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement coverage: direct yielded receivers and computed keys can stage across multiple expression-statement suspensions before assignment, prefix/postfix update, or `delete` side effects, preserving receiver-then-key ordering and final-resume timing; broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement RHS coverage: a direct yielded assignment RHS can stage after the yielded receiver and computed key, preserving receiver/key/RHS order before final mutation; broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement nested-RHS coverage: an expression-statement assignment can stage a supported non-assignment binary RHS expression tree containing multiple direct yields after the yielded receiver and computed key, preserving receiver/key/RHS order before final mutation; broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement nested-assignment-RHS coverage: an expression-statement assignment can recursively stage nested assignment RHS graphs when each nested lvalue uses supported yielded receiver/key operands and each RHS uses direct yields, preserving source-order suspension and final mutation timing; deeper or unsupported assignment graphs remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement unary-RHS coverage: an expression-statement assignment can stage supported prefix/unary RHS expressions over direct yields after the yielded receiver and computed key, preserving receiver/key/RHS order before final mutation; unsupported unary operators and broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement literal-RHS coverage: an expression-statement assignment can stage nested array/object literals with direct yielded element and property values after the yielded receiver and computed key, preserving receiver/key/RHS order before final mutation; spreads, typed computed fields, yielded accessor bodies, and broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement terminal-spread-RHS coverage: nested array/object RHS literals can stage one direct yielded spread source when it is the final yield-bearing literal operand, materializing the spread before a later outer yield and preserving spread side effects and final mutation order; non-final or multiple spreads, unsupported iterable shapes, and broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield assignment computed-key coverage: dynamic-object terminal and expression-statement assignment RHS literals can stage supported computed-property key trees containing finite direct yields, including bounded yield-free call/new wrappers, after the final key yield and before later value yields; typed computed fields and broader key/object-assignment graphs remain deferred. Tests: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_assignment_return`, `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement method-RHS coverage: expression-statement assignments can stage dynamic object RHS literals with static-key, yield-free methods and non-async generator methods, including generic control-flow bodies handled by the existing generator closure path, alongside yielded property values while preserving method closure construction and later invocation; async generator methods, yielded accessor bodies, and broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator multi-yield mutation-statement accessor-RHS coverage: expression-statement assignments can stage dynamic object RHS literals with static-key, yield-free getters and setters alongside yielded property values, preserving accessor descriptor creation and post-resume getter/setter behavior; yielded accessor bodies and broader assignment expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator nested mutation-statement receiver coverage: a yielded receiver can stage with a yielded computed key before a final static member postfix update, preserving receiver/key ordering; calls, intermediate side-effecting member steps, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate mutation-member coverage: a static member between a yielded receiver and a later yielded computed key is evaluated and stored after the receiver resumes, preserving getter timing before the key suspension; calls, computed intermediate members, deeper member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal intermediate mutation-member coverage: a static member between a yielded receiver and a later yielded computed key in a terminal prefix/postfix mutation is evaluated before the key suspension and reused during final completion; calls, computed intermediate members, deeper member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate computed-member coverage: one yielded or stable computed key can resolve an intermediate element access after its key resumes and before a later yielded key, preserving dynamic getter timing across expression-statement mutation chains; calls, deeper computed/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal intermediate computed-member coverage: one yielded or stable computed key can resolve an intermediate element access before a later yielded key in a terminal prefix/postfix mutation, preserving dynamic getter timing through final completion; calls, deeper computed/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-call coverage: a yield-free supported method call on a yielded receiver can run before a later mutation key yield, preserving call side effects and returned-receiver ordering; calls with spread or nested/non-direct yielded arguments, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal intermediate supported-call coverage: a yield-free supported method call on a yielded receiver can run before a later mutation key yield in a terminal prefix mutation, preserving call side effects and returned-receiver ordering through final completion; calls with spread or nested/non-direct yielded arguments, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-call-chain coverage: an ordered chain of yield-free supported method calls can stage each returned receiver before a later mutation key yield, preserving call side effects and source order across expression-statement mutation chains; calls with spread or nested/non-direct yielded arguments, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal intermediate supported-call-chain coverage: an ordered chain of yield-free supported method calls can stage each returned receiver before a later mutation key yield in a terminal prefix mutation, preserving call side effects and source order through final completion; calls with spread or nested/non-direct yielded arguments, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-call-argument coverage: supported method calls in mutation receiver chains can stage direct yielded arguments after the yielded receiver and before a later mutation key yield, preserving argument order and call side effects across expression statements; spread positions outside the covered `call`/`splice`/`concat`/`push`/`unshift` forms, nested/non-direct yielded arguments, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal intermediate supported-call-argument coverage: supported method calls in terminal mutation receiver chains can stage direct yielded arguments after the yielded receiver and before a later mutation key yield, preserving argument order and call side effects through final completion; spread positions outside the covered `call`/`splice`/`concat`/`push`/`unshift` forms, nested/non-direct yielded arguments, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-spread-call coverage: a supported `splice` mutation receiver can stage a direct yielded spread list after its fixed `start` and `deleteCount` arguments and before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, receiver mutation, and returned-receiver side effects in expression statements; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal supported-spread-call coverage: a supported `splice` mutation receiver can stage a direct yielded spread list after its fixed `start` and `deleteCount` arguments and before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, receiver mutation, and returned-receiver side effects through terminal completion; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-concat-spread coverage: a supported `concat` mutation receiver can stage a direct yielded spread list before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, returned-array construction, and returned-receiver side effects in expression statements; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal supported-concat-spread coverage: a supported `concat` mutation receiver can stage a direct yielded spread list before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, returned-array construction, and returned-receiver side effects through terminal completion; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-push-spread coverage: supported `push` mutation receivers can stage a direct yielded spread list before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, receiver mutation, and call side effects in expression statements; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal supported-unshift-spread coverage: supported `unshift` mutation receivers can stage a direct yielded spread list before a later mutation key yield, preserving reverse insertion order, receiver mutation, and call side effects through terminal completion; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator intermediate supported-call-spread coverage: dynamic `Function.prototype.call` mutation receivers can stage a direct yielded spread list after the `thisArg` and before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, invocation side effects, and returned-receiver mutation in expression statements; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_statement`.

- Verified bounded lazy-generator terminal supported-call-spread coverage: dynamic `Function.prototype.call` mutation receivers can stage a direct yielded spread list after the `thisArg` and before a later mutation key yield, preserving spread order, invocation side effects, and returned-receiver mutation through terminal completion; other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader terminal mutation expressions remain deferred. Test: `generator_lazy_multi_yield_mutation_return`.

- Verified bounded synchronous `using` coverage: ordinary local block lists can bind dynamic disposable values, including async functions without suspension in the using scope, and dispose them in reverse declaration order on normal completion, after direct or nested local `return`/`throw` expressions have been evaluated, or on direct loop-body `break`/`continue` exits through `Symbol.dispose`; nested loop-control exits remain deferred. Test: `using_dispose`.

- Verified bounded `Symbol.dispose` coverage: the well-known symbol now has stable singleton identity, description, static-emitter support, and dynamic symbol-key lookup, providing the synchronous counterpart needed by explicit resource management. Test: `symbols`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle disposal coverage: `fs.promises.open` handles now expose `Symbol.asyncDispose`, which delegates to asynchronous `close()`, fulfills after disposal, rejects later I/O, and remains idempotent on repeated disposal. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_async_dispose`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle write coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `writeFile()` for current-position string/Buffer writes, UTF-8/hex/base64 encodings, optional `flush: true`, and closed-handle rejection without replacing or closing the caller-owned descriptor. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_write_file`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle read coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `readFile()` with default/explicit Buffer results, UTF-8/hex/base64 string encodings, current-position reads, and closed-handle rejection without closing the caller-owned descriptor. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_file`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle append coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `appendFile()` for string/Buffer data, UTF-8/hex/base64 encodings, optional `flush: true`, and closed-handle rejection without closing the caller-owned descriptor. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_append_file`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle metadata-mutation coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `chmod()`, `chown()`, and `utimes()` with numeric/Date arguments and reject after asynchronous `close()`. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_metadata`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle vector I/O coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `readv()` and `writev()` over `Buffer[]` arrays with numeric/null positions, Node-shaped byte-count/buffer-array results, and closed-handle rejection. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_readv_writev`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle durability coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support ordered `sync()` and `datasync()` requests and reject after asynchronous `close()`. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_sync`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle mutation coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `truncate(len?)` with a default zero length and reject after asynchronous `close()`. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_truncate`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle metadata coverage: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now expose `stat()` with typed `FSStats` results and reject after asynchronous `close()`. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_stat`.

- Verified bounded async FileHandle I/O coverage: the libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handle now reads and writes Buffer ranges with offset, length, and position arguments, returns Node-shaped byte-count/buffer result objects, and preserves asynchronous close behavior. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_io`.

- Verified bounded `fs.promises.open` coverage: libuv-backed open now returns a `FileHandle` value with a read-only `fd` and asynchronous `close()` method, preserving the existing string/numeric open-flag and mode contract. Test: `fs_promises_open`.

- Verified bounded async `for await...of` terminal-throw coverage: a supported iterator continuation now propagates a synchronous `throw` after iterator exhaustion, including sync-source adaptation, while existing iterator return/throw and close behavior remains green. Test: `async_for_await_terminal_throw`.

- Verified bounded async-from-sync `for await...of` coverage: the specialized async iterator continuation now adapts synchronous arrays, strings, and symbol-backed iterables, awaits promise-valued sync iterator results, forwards early-return `IteratorClose`, and preserves the existing bare `next()` async-iterator compatibility path. Tests: `async_for_await_sync_iterables`, `async_for_await_sync_iterator_close`; existing `async_for_await_events_on_` family remains green.

- Verified empty synchronous terminal-finally return coverage: ordinary awaited loops (with and without catch) and nested iterator bodies now settle `finally { return; }` as `undefined` after awaited cleanup, while cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_finally_empty_return`, `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_terminal_finally_empty_return`.

- Verified counted awaited loop-body synchronous terminal-finally parity: a counted `for` preserves its awaited condition and incrementor while final synchronous `finally` `return`/`throw` overrides source and catch terminal completions; cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_finally_synchronous_return`, `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_finally_synchronous_throw`.

- Verified iterator nested awaited-if catch-plus-synchronous-terminal-finally coverage: both `for...of` and `for...in` carry source and awaited catch terminal completions through awaited cleanup to a final synchronous `finally` `return` or `throw`, while cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_catch_finally_synchronous_return`, `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_catch_finally_synchronous_throw`.

- Verified iterator nested awaited-if synchronous terminal-finally override coverage: both `for...of` and `for...in` bodies apply a final synchronous `finally` `return` or `throw` after awaited cleanup, overriding source terminal completion while cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_terminal_finally_synchronous_return`, `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_terminal_finally_synchronous_throw`.

- Verified ordinary awaited loop-body synchronous terminal-finally override coverage: after awaited cleanup, a final `finally` `return` or `throw` overrides source and catch terminal completions; cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_finally_synchronous_return`, `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_finally_synchronous_throw`, `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_catch_finally_synchronous_return`, `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_catch_finally_synchronous_throw`.

- Verified counted awaited loop-body terminal-finally override parity: a counted `for` preserves its awaited condition and incrementor while a final `finally` `return await` or `throw await` overrides source and catch terminal completions; final-terminal rejection propagates and cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_finally_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_finally_terminal_await_throw`, `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_catch_finally_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_catch_finally_terminal_await_throw`.

- Verified ordinary awaited loop-body terminal catch-completion coverage: a source `try/catch/finally` can sequence awaited catch recovery and finish the catch with terminal `return await` or `throw await`, carrying that completion through multiple awaited `finally` cleanups; catch-recovery rejection, catch-terminal rejection, and cleanup rejection preserve JavaScript precedence. Tests: `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_catch_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_catch_terminal_await_throw`.

- Verified ordinary awaited loop-body terminal-finally override coverage: a source terminal `return await` or `throw await` can carry its completion through awaited cleanup and then let a final `finally` `return await` or `throw await` override it; source rejection is overridden after fulfilled cleanup, final-terminal rejection propagates, and cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_finally_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_finally_terminal_await_throw`.

- Verified ordinary awaited loop-body catch-plus-terminal-finally coverage: source terminal completion, catch recovery rejection, and catch terminal rejection all flow through awaited cleanup to a final `finally` `return await` or `throw await` override; final-terminal rejection propagates and cleanup rejection retains precedence. Tests: `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_catch_finally_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_loop_body_await_try_terminal_catch_finally_terminal_await_throw`.

- Verified counted awaited loop-body terminal catch-completion parity: a counted `for` preserves its awaited condition and incrementor while sequencing awaited catch recovery and finishing with terminal `return await` or `throw await`; source-stage rejection, source-terminal rejection, catch-recovery rejection, catch-terminal rejection, and cleanup rejection preserve JavaScript precedence. Tests: `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_catch_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_for_body_await_try_terminal_catch_terminal_await_throw`.

- Verified iterator nested awaited-if terminal catch-completion coverage: both `for...of` and `for...in` bodies can sequence awaited catch recovery and finish the catch with terminal `return await` or `throw await`, carrying that completion through awaited `finally` cleanup; source-stage rejection, source-terminal rejection, catch-recovery rejection, catch-terminal rejection, and cleanup rejection preserve JavaScript precedence. Tests: `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_catch_terminal_await_return`, `async_await_iterator_body_multi_await_nested_await_if_catch_terminal_await_throw`.

- **Phase 6 — `async`/`await` + libuv event loop**
  - Verified bounded extension: ordered awaited locals can be carried through await-free statements between suspensions and subsequent awaited body work before `continue`; broader alias sets and loop-body multi-suspension graphs remain part of the remaining state-machine work.
  - Verified adjacent nested-branch coverage: an awaited loop-body `if` branch now has regression proof for multiple awaited locals, await-free interstitial statements, and later awaited body work before `continue`; broader nested branch graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified assigned-alias extension: an assigned-before-use `let` local now carries its awaited value through multiple direct loop-body suspensions before `continue`; broader assignment and nested-control-flow graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified nested assigned-alias coverage: nested awaited loop branches reuse the same assigned-before-use continuation environment across multiple awaits before `continue`; broader nested branch and break/return graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified break fallthrough coverage: direct awaited assigned aliases survive multiple body suspensions into `break` and the post-loop awaited return; broader nested break/return graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified nested break extension: a suspending `continue` arm and an assigned-alias multi-await `break` arm now compose through the conditional loop adapter; broader nested break/return graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified nested return-prelude extension: declaration- and assignment-form awaited locals with await-free interstitial statements now compose before a nested final `return await`.
  - Verified terminal alias extension: nested final `return await` expressions can read assigned/declaration awaited locals carried through the prelude chain; broader terminal return graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified iterator terminal binding extension: bounded awaited `for...of` and `for...in` return arms carry the current iteration value into terminal `return await` expressions; broader iterator state-machine graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified iterator awaited-local extension: bounded awaited `for...of` and `for...in` return/throw arms carry one assigned-before-use awaited local into terminal expressions; broader iterator local/control-flow graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified iterator multi-await extension: bounded `for...of` and `for...in` bodies can sequence multiple direct awaited statements before terminal `return await`/`throw await`, carrying initialized awaited locals across the sequence; broader iterator local/control-flow graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified iterator assigned multi-await extension: bounded iterator sequences carry assigned-before-use awaited locals across multiple body suspensions into terminal expressions; broader iterator local/control-flow graphs remain deferred.
  - Verified iterator postlude extension: bounded iterator multi-await sequences execute supported await-free expression statements, initialized declarations, `if`/`else` including assigned-before-use branch-local `let`/`var` declarations, `switch` including clause fallthrough and assigned-before-use `let`/`var` clause locals, `while`, `do…while`, counted-`for` including declaration-bearing top-level postlude loops, top-level and nested iterator `for…of`/`for…in` loops with `continue`/`break`, nested ordinary `while`/`do…while`/counted-`for` loops with `continue`/`break`, `try`/`catch`/`finally` including assigned-before-use catch `var` locals, and `try`/`finally` postludes between suspensions before terminal expressions; broader iterator control-flow postludes remain deferred.
  - Verified ordinary awaited iterator-loop body extension: `for…of` and `for…in` bodies can carry assigned-before-use `var` aliases across two direct awaits before `continue` or `break`, including bounded await-free `if` routing with await-free branch preludes and explicit or implicit fallthrough, a direct awaited branch condition, simple non-fallthrough or empty/nonterminal-clause-fallthrough `switch` clauses, a direct awaited switch discriminator, bounded fourteen-await `if`-arm or `switch`-clause control sequences with initialized or assigned-before-use simple control-arm prefix locals, multiple initialized or assigned-before-use awaited control-arm aliases, initialized or assigned-before-use simple locals declared between control-arm suspensions, synchronous valued and empty `return`, terminal `return await`, terminal `throw await`, and synchronous `throw` control arms, plus await-free interstitial and post-await statements, to those outer-loop controls; broader loop-body alias and suspension graphs remain deferred.

## 2. Dependent phases

- **Dispatch concurrency (optional, libdispatch-backed) — remainder** (design/status: [`dispatch_async_todo.md`](dispatch_async_todo.md))
  - Remaining work: `Shared<T>`/queue-confinement capture escape hatches beyond const primitives; broader task return types beyond number/string/boolean/array/Map/Set/Date/RegExp/Error/Buffer/URL/URLSearchParams/ArrayBuffer/DataView/TextEncoder/TextDecoder/void; relaxing the awaited-shape dependence once Phase 6 general state-machine lowering lands (dispatch promises awaited outside the recognized continuation subsets currently hit the pending-await bail); and a many-thread GC soak + TSan CI configuration and a CI job that builds libdispatch from source.

- **Phase 7 remainder — ES language features**

  - Phase 8 refinement: dynamic `Function.prototype.call` mutation receivers now accept a direct yielded spread list after the `thisArg` and before a later mutation-key yield in both expression-statement and terminal prefix-return paths; spread order, invocation side effects, and returned-receiver mutation are preserved. Other spread positions/methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, unsupported methods, deeper call/member graphs, and broader mutation expressions remain deferred.
  - Remaining Phase 8 call-spread work: support additional spread positions and methods, nested/non-direct yielded spreads, and broader call/member mutation graphs.

  - Phase 8 terminal-assignment refinement: terminal assignments now accept supported non-assignment RHS expression trees containing multiple direct yields after staged receiver/key operands; nested assignment RHS graphs with supported yielded receiver/key operands and direct-yield RHS values are covered by the recursive nested-assignment refinement, while deeper or unsupported lvalue/assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 terminal nested-assignment refinement: terminal assignments now recursively stage bounded nested assignment RHS graphs with supported yielded receiver/key operands and direct-yield RHS values, preserving source-order suspension and final assignment results; deeper or unsupported lvalue/assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 terminal-assignment spread-RHS refinement: terminal assignment RHS literals now stage one direct yielded spread source when it is the final yield-bearing literal operand, preserving spread side effects before later outer yields; non-final or multiple spreads and broader lvalue/assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 dynamic computed-key refinement: terminal and expression-statement assignment RHS literals now stage supported dynamic-object computed-property key trees containing finite direct yields, including bounded yield-free call/new wrappers, after the final key yield and before later value yields; typed computed fields and broader key graphs remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 expression-statement assignment refinement: mutation statements now accept supported non-assignment binary RHS expression trees containing multiple direct yields after staged receiver/key operands; nested assignment RHS graphs with supported yielded receiver/key operands and direct-yield RHS values are covered by the recursive nested-assignment refinement, while deeper or unsupported assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 expression-statement nested-assignment refinement: mutation statements now recursively stage bounded nested assignment RHS graphs with supported yielded receiver/key operands and direct-yield RHS values, preserving source-order suspension and final mutation timing; deeper or unsupported assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 unary-RHS refinement: mutation statements now accept supported prefix/unary RHS expressions over direct yields after staged receiver/key operands; unsupported unary operators and broader assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 literal-RHS refinement: mutation statements now accept nested no-spread array/object RHS literals with direct yielded element/property values after staged receiver/key operands; spreads, typed computed fields, yielded accessor bodies, and broader assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 final-spread-RHS refinement: nested array/object RHS literals now stage one direct yielded spread source when it is the final yield-bearing literal operand, preserving spread side effects before later outer yields; non-final or multiple spreads and broader assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 method-RHS refinement: mutation statement RHS object literals now accept static-key, yield-free methods and non-async generator methods, including generic control-flow bodies handled by the existing generator closure path, alongside yielded property values while preserving method closure construction and later invocation; async generator methods, yielded accessor bodies, and broader assignment expressions remain deferred.
  - Phase 8 accessor-RHS refinement: mutation statement RHS object literals now accept static-key, yield-free getters and setters alongside yielded property values, preserving descriptor creation and post-resume access; yielded accessor bodies and broader assignment expressions remain deferred.

- **Phase 9 — `Proxy` + `Reflect`**
  - Verified top-level dynamic `JSON.stringify` semantics: `undefined`, functions, and callable proxies now produce JavaScript `undefined`, while nested arrays retain `null` substitution, objects omit unsupported properties, and revoked callable proxies still throw. Tests: `json_stringify_dynamic_top_level`, `proxy_callable_json`, `proxy_nested_callable_identity`, `proxy_revocable_identity`.
  - Verified bounded async source-try selectors: unshadowed `Reflect` calls now accept a direct awaited target with await-free trailing arguments, covering `has`, `get`, `set`, `getOwnPropertyDescriptor`, and `isExtensible` truthiness, target mutation, synchronous target errors, awaited rejection, and `finally` cleanup. Test: `async_await_branch_block_source_try_conditional_awaited_reflect_target`.
  - Verified bounded async source-try Proxy selectors: `new Proxy(await target, handler)` and `Proxy.revocable(await target, handler)` now preserve proxy creation, null-target validation, awaited rejection, and synchronous `finally` cleanup across declarations, class methods, and async arrows. Test: `async_await_branch_block_source_try_conditional_awaited_proxy_target`.
  - Verified the bounded Proxy/Reflect audit in no-GC mode (112/112 filtered cases pass) and normal-GC mode (110/110 `proxy_` cases pass), including `proxy_descriptor_invariants`, after proxy target/handler slots gained raw GC-visible roots alongside their tagged values; focused no-GC descriptor/invariant checks also pass, and revocation clears the handler root. The broader Proxy/Reflect TODO remains open.
  - Verified bounded async source-try Promise static selectors: supported unshadowed `Promise` statics now accept a direct awaited first operand with await-free trailing arguments, including `resolve` and the array combinators; fulfillment, awaited source rejection, and synchronous `finally` cleanup remain correct across declarations, class methods, and async arrows. Test: `async_await_branch_block_source_try_conditional_awaited_promise_statics`.
  - Remaining work: broader ECMAScript Proxy edge-case validation beyond the currently covered callable/newable/object/array/function identity and forwarding paths, revocation paths, handler and inherited-trap lookup, nullish/accessor trap forwarding and validation, receiver identity, Object helper / JSON / Object.assign integration, own-key and descriptor result validation, non-configurable/non-extensible target invariants, array/function/prototype/integrity invariant subsets, and construct `newTarget` identity/default/prototype paths.

---

## 3. Broad surface areas

- **Phases 11–13 — async Node stdlib**
  - Phase 11 encoding checkpoint: `fs.readFileSync` and libuv-backed `fs.promises.readFile` now accept direct and object-literal ASCII, Latin-1/binary encodings, with focused normal-GC and no-GC coverage. Test: `fs_read_file_additional_encodings`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle encoding checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now accept direct and object-literal ASCII, Latin-1, and binary `readFile()` encodings while preserving existing Buffer/null/UTF-8/hex/base64 behavior. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_file_additional_encodings`.
  - Phase 11 write/append encoding checkpoint: sync and libuv-backed Promise `fs.writeFile` / `appendFile` plus libuv-backed FileHandle `writeFile()` / `appendFile()` now accept direct and object-literal ASCII, Latin-1, and binary string encodings while preserving existing UTF-8/hex/base64 and Buffer behavior. Test: `fs_write_append_additional_encodings`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle cancellation checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now accept `readFile({ signal })`, reject pending and pre-aborted reads, and preserve the caller-owned descriptor for explicit close. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_file_abort`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle cancellation checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now accept `writeFile({ signal })` and `appendFile({ signal })`, reject pending and pre-aborted writes, and preserve the caller-owned descriptor for explicit close. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_write_append_abort`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle I/O options checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now accept `{ offset, length, position }` options objects for Buffer `read()` and `write()`, including nullable current-position reads and default lengths derived from the buffer offset. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_io_options`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle read-options checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now accept the options-only `read({ buffer?, offset?, length?, position? })` form, including caller-provided and zero-filled 16 KiB default Buffers. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_options`.
  - Phase 11 DNS checkpoint: `dns.resolve4` / `resolve6` and their Promise forms now accept literal `{ ttl: true }` options and return `{ address, ttl }` records, parsing DNS answer TTLs when available and using deterministic `ttl: 0` host-resolver fallback records. Test: `dns_resolve_ttl`.
  - Phase 11 fs directory-handle checkpoint: `fs.opendirSync(...)` and `fs.promises.opendir(...)` now expose bounded POSIX handles with synchronous/Promise reads, boxed `Dirent` entries, close methods, and direct async-iterator protocol methods. Test: `fs_opendir`.
  - Phase 11 fs recursive directory-handle checkpoint: `fs.opendirSync(...)` and `fs.promises.opendir(...)` now accept literal/const `{ recursive: true }` options for depth-first traversal with early close of active and parent handles. Test: `fs_opendir_recursive`.
  - Phase 11 fs directory-entry encoding checkpoint: `fs.opendirSync(...)` and `fs.promises.opendir(...)` now accept literal/const UTF-8, hex, base64, and Buffer `encoding` options for returned names. Test: `fs_opendir_encoding`.
  - Phase 11 fs directory buffer-size checkpoint: `fs.opendirSync(...)` and `fs.promises.opendir(...)` now accept literal/const `bufferSize` options with the Node default of 32 and range 1 through 4,294,967,295; active recursive directory frames prefetch no more than the configured number of raw names. Test: `fs_opendir_buffer_size`.

  - Phase 11 cancellation checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.readdir` shallow, recursive, encoded, and Dirent scanners now reject queued/in-flight requests on AbortSignal cancellation, ask the active scanner to cancel, and suppress late fulfillment. Test: `fs_promises_readdir_abort_libuv`.
  - Phase 11 cancellation checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.stat` / `lstat` and `realpath` / `readlink` / `mkdtemp` now reject queued/in-flight requests on AbortSignal cancellation, ask the active request to cancel, and suppress late fulfillment. Test: `fs_promises_metadata_abort_libuv`.
  - Phase 11 cancellation checkpoint: signal-bearing libuv-backed `fs.promises.mkdir`, `rm`, and `rmdir` requests, including recursive state machines, now reject queued/in-flight requests on AbortSignal cancellation, ask the active request to cancel, and suppress late fulfillment.   - Phase 11 cancellation checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.access` and `statfs` requests now accept bounded signal options, reject queued/in-flight requests on AbortSignal cancellation, ask the active request to cancel, and suppress late fulfillment. Test: `fs_promises_access_statfs_abort_libuv`.
  - Phase 11 options checkpoint: `fs.writeFileSync` / `appendFileSync` and libuv-backed `fs.promises.writeFile` / `appendFile` now honor literal `{ flush: true }` through `fflush`/`fsync` or `uv_fs_fsync` before close. Test: `fs_write_append_flush`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support ordered asynchronous `sync()` and `datasync()` durability requests, and closed handles reject later sync requests. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_sync`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support asynchronous `truncate(len?)`, including the default zero length, and closed handles reject later truncation requests. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_truncate`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now expose asynchronous `stat()` with typed `FSStats` results, and closed handles reject later `stat()` calls. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_stat`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support Buffer `read()` and `write()` requests with bounded offset/length/position arguments and `{ bytesRead, buffer }` / `{ bytesWritten, buffer }` results, followed by asynchronous `close()`. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_io`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `readv()` and `writev()` over `Buffer[]` arrays with numeric/null positions and `{ bytesRead, buffers }` / `{ bytesWritten, buffers }` results, while closed handles reject later vector I/O requests. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_readv_writev`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support asynchronous `chmod()`, `chown()`, and `utimes()` descriptor mutations, including Date timestamp values, while closed handles reject later metadata requests. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_metadata`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `appendFile()` for string/Buffer data, UTF-8/hex/base64 encodings, optional `flush: true`, and closed-handle rejection while preserving the caller-owned descriptor. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_append_file`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `writeFile()` for current-position string/Buffer writes, UTF-8/hex/base64 encodings, optional `flush: true`, and closed-handle rejection without replacing or closing the caller-owned descriptor. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_write_file`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now support `readFile()` with default/explicit Buffer results, UTF-8/hex/base64 string encodings, current-position reads, and closed-handle rejection without closing the caller-owned descriptor. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_file`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now expose `Symbol.asyncDispose`, delegating to asynchronous `close()`, fulfilling after disposal, rejecting later I/O, and remaining idempotent on repeated disposal. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_async_dispose`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now accept string `write(data, position?, encoding?)` calls through the existing asynchronous byte-write path and return the original string in the write result. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_string_write`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle checkpoint: libuv-backed `fs.promises.open` handles now expose a bounded `readLines()` async iterator with CRLF/CR/LF splitting, direct `next()` / `return()` calls, `[Symbol.asyncIterator]()` identity, dynamic `for await...of` consumption, inclusive `start` / `end` byte ranges that preserve the caller-owned descriptor position, UTF-8/ASCII/Latin-1/binary/hex/base64 encodings, a bounded `close` event, and configurable `highWaterMark` read chunks. Tests: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines`, `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_range`, `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_encoding`, `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_emit_close`, `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_high_water_mark`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle auto-close checkpoint: `readLines({ autoClose })` now closes the underlying descriptor by default when the iterator exhausts, is returned, or fails, while `autoClose: false` preserves the caller-owned descriptor for later use. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_autoclose`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle emit-close checkpoint: `readLines({ emitClose })` now exposes EventEmitter-compatible iterator listeners and emits one `close` event on exhaustion, return, or read failure by default, while `emitClose: false` suppresses that event. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_emit_close`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle high-water-mark checkpoint: `readLines({ highWaterMark })` now validates non-negative safe-integer chunk sizes, defaults to 64 KiB, accepts zero, and routes the selected size through the existing libuv reader. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_high_water_mark`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle cancellation checkpoint: `readLines({ signal })` now rejects pending iterator `next()` calls with the AbortSignal reason and handles pre-aborted signals. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_abort`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle range checkpoint: `readLines({ start, end })` now uses inclusive positional byte ranges, accepts either bound independently, rejects invalid or reversed ranges, and leaves the caller-owned descriptor position unchanged. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_range`.
  - Phase 11 FileHandle encoding checkpoint: `readLines({ encoding })` now accepts UTF-8, ASCII, Latin-1/binary, hex, and base64 encodings, preserving decoded line splitting for byte-oriented encodings and encoded output for hex/base64. Test: `fs_promises_file_handle_read_lines_encoding`.
  - Phase 11 scheduling checkpoint: `timers/promises.setInterval` now returns a bounded async iterator that yields configured values through direct `next()` and dynamic `for await...of`, closes on iterator return/break, and rejects once before completing after AbortSignal cancellation. Test: `timers_promises_set_interval`.
