- Phase 12 chunked transport checkpoint: `http` / `node:http` now decode chunked request bodies and expose request/response `trailers`, emit validated `addTrailers(...)` fields on chunked requests/responses, and encode buffered client/server bodies by default when `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` is selected. Explicit request/response streaming and server keep-alive are tracked separately; chunked keep-alive reuse and general streaming/backpressure remain deferred. Tests: `http_chunked_transport`, `http_trailers`.
- Phase 12 keep-alive checkpoint: `http.createServer(requestListener?)` now processes sequential requests with explicit `Connection: keep-alive` and `Content-Length` bodies on one native TCP connection, preserves unread pipelined bytes, emits `Connection: keep-alive` responses, and closes after a final `Connection: close` request. Concurrent pooling, chunked keep-alive reuse, and general streaming/backpressure remain deferred. Test: `http_keep_alive`.
- Phase 12 chunked streaming checkpoint: explicit `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` server responses now flush each `write()` as a framed chunk, and client response objects expose `data` / `end` events while maintaining the compatibility `body` field. Broader keep-alive/chunked pooling and general streaming/backpressure remain deferred. Test: `http_response_streaming`.
- Phase 12 chunked response-backpressure checkpoint: explicit chunked HTTP `ServerResponse.write()` calls now propagate the underlying 16 KiB socket backpressure result, expose synchronized writable high-water state, and forward `drain` through response EventEmitter listeners. Broader HTTP client/request backpressure, chunked keep-alive reuse, and general streaming remain deferred. Test: `http_response_backpressure`.
- Phase 12 chunked request-backpressure checkpoint: explicit chunked HTTP `ClientRequest.write()` calls now report queued 16 KiB backpressure before and after connection, expose synchronized writable high-water state, and forward socket `drain` through request EventEmitter listeners. Broader HTTP pooling, chunked keep-alive reuse, and general streaming remain deferred. Test: `http_request_backpressure`.
- Phase 12 chunked keep-alive checkpoint: sequential framed chunked HTTP requests and responses now reuse one endpoint-keyed idle connection, preserve exact server-side chunk consumption, and discard non-reusable pooled sockets after close-delimited responses. Concurrent pooling, pipelining, and broader streaming remain deferred. Test: `http_chunked_keep_alive_pool`.
- Phase 12 chunked request-streaming checkpoint: explicit `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` client requests now preserve individual `write()` calls as wire-level chunks before the terminating chunk, while ordinary requests remain buffered. Broader keep-alive/chunked pooling and general streaming/backpressure remain deferred. Test: `http_request_streaming`.
- Phase 12 HTTPS server/client checkpoint: `https` / `node:https` `createServer({ key, cert }, requestListener?)` accepts PEM credentials and negotiates server-side OpenSSL TLS over the native nonblocking socket runtime; `request` / `get` negotiate client-side TLS with the bounded HTTP options plus `rejectUnauthorized` and `servername`, reusing the HTTP/1.1 request/response parser. General streaming/backpressure and best-effort `http2` remain deferred. Test: `https_client_transport`.
  - Phase 12 cancellation checkpoint: `http.request` / `http.get` and the shared `https.request` client path accept pre-aborted and in-flight `signal` options, emit the signal reason once as a request error, and preserve normal-GC and no-GC behavior. Broader AbortSignal exception-channel semantics remain deferred. Tests: `http_request_signal`, `https_request_signal`.
  - Phase 12 authentication checkpoint: `http.request` / `http.get` and the shared `https.request` / `https.get` client path accept `auth`, synthesize a Basic `Authorization` header unless an explicit authorization header is supplied, and preserve normal-GC and no-GC behavior. Broader proxy, agent, and credential negotiation semantics remain deferred. Tests: `http_request_auth`, `https_client_transport`.
  - Phase 12 client-timeout checkpoint: `http.request` / `http.get` ClientRequest objects now expose chainable `setTimeout(timeout, callback?)`, forward the bounded native socket idle timer, emit `timeout`, and clear the forwarding bridge on response completion or destruction. Broader agent, proxy, and full stream-timeout semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_timeout`.
  - Phase 12 client-socket-options checkpoint: `http.request` / `http.get` ClientRequest objects now expose chainable `setNoDelay(noDelay?)` and `setSocketKeepAlive(enable?, initialDelay?)`, forwarding the existing native POSIX socket controls and validation. Broader agent, proxy, and full socket-option semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_socket_options`.
  - Phase 12 client-abort checkpoint: `http.request` / `http.get` ClientRequest objects now expose one-shot chainable `abort()`, synchronize `aborted`, emit `abort`, and reuse the existing native destroy lifecycle. Broader abort and agent semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_abort`.
  - Phase 12 client-destroy checkpoint: `http.request` / `http.get` ClientRequest objects now preserve a supplied `destroy(error?, callback?)` value, emit `error` before teardown, invoke callbacks after teardown including on repeated calls, and remain chainable. Broader agent and stream-lifecycle semantics remain deferred. Test: `http_request_destroy_error`.
  - Phase 12 client transport checkpoint: `http.request(options, callback?)` and `http.get(options, callback?)` now send bounded HTTP/1.1 requests over native TCP, support hostname/host/port/path/method/headers options, buffered string/Buffer writes, auto-ending `get`, response status/version/headers/body metadata, and bounded sequential same-origin keep-alive pooling for ordinary non-chunked requests. Concurrent pooling, chunked keep-alive reuse, streaming, and best-effort `http2` remain deferred. Tests: `http_client_transport`, `http_client_keep_alive_pool`.
  - Phase 12 metadata checkpoint: `http` / `node:http` named, namespace, and default imports now expose the complete `METHODS` array, complete `STATUS_CODES` map, and `maxHeaderSize`; broader keep-alive/chunked pooling, streaming behavior, and best-effort `http2` remain deferred. Test: `http_metadata`.
  - Phase 12 validation checkpoint: `http` / `node:http` named, namespace, and default imports now validate token names and header content through `validateHeaderName` and `validateHeaderValue`, including optional-label and ignored-argument evaluation; broader keep-alive/chunked pooling, streaming behavior, and best-effort `http2` remain deferred. Test: `http_header_validation`.
- Phase 12 HTTP transport checkpoint: `http.createServer(requestListener?)` now provides bounded HTTP/1.1 request parsing and response writing over the native TCP runtime, including request metadata, lower-case headers, `Content-Length` bodies, response status/headers/body methods, and close lifecycle coverage. Broader chunked keep-alive, concurrent pooling, streaming, and best-effort `http2` remain deferred. Test: `http_server_transport`.
  - Phase 13 checkpoint: `child_process.spawn(file, args?, options?)` now creates real asynchronous POSIX child handles with pipe-backed stdin/stdout/stderr, a live `stdio` alias array, dynamic EventEmitter-compatible `.on` / `.once` / removal methods, `data` / `end` stream events, `spawn` / `exit` / `close` lifecycle events, failed-launch `error` delivery without `spawn`, stream `setEncoding("utf8")`, stdin `write` / `end`, `argv0` forwarding plus POSIX-inert Windows compatibility options, `spawnfile` / `spawnargs` launch metadata, and `kill` / exit-signal state across named, namespace, and default module forms; see `child_process_spawn`, `child_process_spawn_error`, `child_process_spawn_metadata`, `child_process_spawn_stdio`, `child_process_spawn_options`, `child_process_spawn_streams`, and `child_process_spawn_kill`.
  - Phase 13 stream checkpoint: asynchronous `child_process.spawn` piped stdin now uses a nonblocking 16 KiB queue, exposes writable high-water state, invokes queued write/end callbacks, emits `drain` / `finish`, and reports one error for closed or failed pipes; stdout/stderr pipes implement bounded `pause()` / `resume()` read gates with synchronized readable state, `destroy(error?, callback?)` error/callback delivery, one `close` event after EOF or destruction, and nonblocking `read(size?)` pulls. Broader async child lifecycle, spawn-option, stream backpressure, and read-error semantics remain deferred. Tests: `child_process_spawn_stream_backpressure`, `child_process_spawn_stream_pause_resume`, `child_process_spawn_stream_destroy`, `child_process_spawn_stream_close`, `child_process_spawn_stream_read`.
  - Phase 13 pipe checkpoint: asynchronous `child_process.spawn` stdout/stderr streams now forward data through bounded one-destination `pipe()` / `unpipe()` support, return the destination, honor `{ end: false }`, end destinations by default, and resume pipe-paused sources on destination `drain`. Broader multi-destination, lifecycle, and stream backpressure semantics remain deferred. Test: `child_process_spawn_stream_pipe`.
  - Phase 13 lifecycle checkpoint: asynchronous `child_process.spawn` `ref()` / `unref()` now toggle whether child polling and timeout handles keep the runtime event loop alive, allowing unrefed children to outlive the parent’s refed work while `ref()` restores the default keep-alive behavior. Broader async child lifecycle semantics remain deferred. Test: `child_process_spawn_ref_unref`.
  - Phase 13 cancellation checkpoint: asynchronous `child_process.spawn` now accepts an `AbortSignal` option, kills the child with the configured `killSignal`, emits the signal reason before `exit` / `close`, suppresses `spawn` for pre-aborted signals, and preserves `killed`; broader AbortSignal exception-channel and cancellation semantics remain deferred. Test: `child_process_spawn_signal`.
  - Phase 13 timeout checkpoint: asynchronous `child_process.spawn` now accepts a numeric `timeout` option, terminates unfinished POSIX children with the configured `killSignal`, reports `killed`, and cancels the one-shot timer when the child closes. Broader async lifecycle and spawn-option semantics remain deferred. Test: `child_process_spawn_timeout`.
  - Phase 13 remaining work: broader async child-process lifecycle semantics and stream read/backpressure/error handling beyond the covered piped-stdin write, stdout/stderr pause/resume, stream-destroy, stream-close, and pull-read subsets; broader `spawn` / `spawnSync` options beyond the covered cwd/env/shell/stdio/detached/uid/gid/killSignal subset; `fork`; `cluster`; and `worker_threads` with structured-clone messages.

- **Phase 14 — npm integration**
  - Verified bounded CommonJS factory-argument alias proofs: local object-returning factories now accept recursively `const`-aliased static `require(...)` arguments, resolve those factory-parameter member reads, and preserve finite named/default/namespace metadata. Test: `node_modules_commonjs_module_exports_factory_argument_alias_object`.

extend the same AOT class path to zero-argument local function declarations and inline arrow/function-expression factories through direct, `Function.call`, `Function.apply`, `Reflect.apply`, and `Function.bind`, corresponding object-preserving wrappers and empty-target/empty-descriptor compositions, zero-argument IIFE return wrappers around top-level aliases, and bounded IIFE-local factory declaration/`const` alias scopes. Broader class-valued wrapper and re-export dependency shapes remain in the Phase 14 item below.
  - Verified direct and object-literal external CommonJS class-valued `allowJs` named-import paths in both normal and no-GC modes. Tests: `node_modules_js_named_import_external_cjs_class`, `node_modules_js_named_import_external_cjs_object_class`.
  - Verified finite CommonJS computed-export metadata for BigInt/null/undefined-derived keys and `as const` key bindings, plus enum-derived finite dynamic-require dispatch, in both normal and no-GC modes. Tests: `node_modules_commonjs_static_computed_bigint`, `node_modules_commonjs_static_computed_null_undefined`, `node_modules_commonjs_computed_keys_as_const`, `dynamic_require_enum`.

Test: `dynamic_require_static_string_well_formed`.

  - Verified finite dynamic-require `matchAll` string-pattern proofs: static string patterns now use the standard global-RegExp conversion and materialize full matches, captures, nested indexing, and collection length. Test: `dynamic_require_static_string_match_all_string`.
  - Verified finite dynamic-require `match` string-pattern proofs: static string patterns now use the standard RegExp conversion and materialize full matches, captures, nested indexing, and collection length. Test: `dynamic_require_static_string_match_string`.

  - Verified finite dynamic-require TextEncoder/TextDecoder module proofs: static UTF-8 encode/decode fragments now accept default and namespace `util` / `node:util` module imports. Test: `dynamic_require_static_text_encoder_module_import`.

  - Verified finite dynamic-require string length proofs: static `.length` reads, including `String(...)` and `String.raw(...)` results, now resolve into the AOT module graph. Test: `dynamic_require_static_string_length`.
  - Verified finite dynamic-require string replacement callback proofs: pure zero-argument `String.prototype.replace()` and `replaceAll()` callbacks with one static return value, including earlier `const` arrow/function-expression aliases and top-level function-declaration identifiers, now resolve into the AOT module graph and lower through the AOT emitter; argument-dependent or mutable callbacks remain deferred. Test: `dynamic_require_static_string_replace_callback`.

Dependency init stays eager through the AOT module graph by design.
